([MCALLERS/fatty-lumpkin-presentation-tool](https://github.com/MCALLERS/fatty-lumpkin-presentation-tool/blob/HEAD/plugins/flpt/agents/flpt-scene-crew.md))
- **flue-architect**: Designs Flue 2 agent systems before any code exists. Give it the user's goal (and the project path); it reads what's already there and returns a structured spec — agents, identities, how each is reached, models, sandboxes, tools, skills, subagents, state, composition, target — plus explicit decisions and open questions. Read-only; writes no code. Use for "I want an agent that…", /flue-loom:compose, or before any non-trivial /flue-loom:new. ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-architect.md))
- **flue-author**: Writes and edits Flue 2 code — 'use agent' modules, tools (plain, harness, durable), SKILL.md skills, subagents, MCP connections, custom hooks, app.ts mounts, and Vitest tests — from a flue-architect spec or a direct change request, following flue-loom's verified templates and Flue's conventions. Use for /flue-loom:new and /flue-loom:compose implementation steps. ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-author.md))
- **flue-debugger**: Diagnoses failing Flue 2 agents — build and agent-scan errors, 404/400 responses, error envelopes, failed or aborted submissions, model/provider errors, tool failures and timeouts, state and delivery surprises, Cloudflare Durable Object issues — by reproducing with flue run or flue-talk, reading the transcript, and returning root cause, minimal fix, and verification. Diagnostic only; does not edit code. Use for /flue-loom:debug or "my Flue agent isn't working". ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-debugger.md))
- **flue-deployer**: Prepares a Flue 2 project for production and returns a deploy plan — target detection, pre-flight checks (lint, types, build, Cloudflare migrations and wiring, secrets, auth, persistence), vite build, wrangler dry run or Node artifact smoke test — applying only safe additive config fixes. It never runs the real deploy; the caller does that after the user confirms. Use for /flue-loom:deploy. ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-deployer.md))
- **flue-explorer**: Maps an existing Flue project fast — target, versions, agents and how each is reached, tools, skills, subagents, MCP connections, sandboxes, models, state, channels, schedules, persistence, auth, tests, and conventions — citing a file for every claim. Read-only apart from diagnostic commands. Use for /flue-loom:explore, when entering an unfamiliar Flue repo, or before designing or reviewing changes. ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-explorer.md))
- **flue-observability**: The outcome is attributable model usage and actionable failures without storing prompt or tool content. Verification requires a real Flue run against a local fixture provider, exact usage totals, safe persisted records, and rejection of incomplete exports. This is local source integration until published to main. ([edmundmiller/dotfiles](https://github.com/edmundmiller/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/flue-observability.md) · ★80)
- **flue-orchestrator**: Implements multi-agent composition in Flue 2 — choosing and writing the delegation mechanism (subagents and the task tool, harness.prompt steps, dispatch()/init() between registered agents, useDispatchMessage, HTTP via @flue/sdk, schedules, durable pipelines) and the code that wires agents together. Use after flue-architect for /flue-loom:compose, or for "make these agents work together". ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-orchestrator.md))
- **flue-reviewer**: Reviews Flue 2 code for correctness, security, and framework best practices — agent modules, hooks, tools, skills, subagents, app.ts routing and auth, persistence, Cloudflare wiring, tests — and reports confidence-filtered findings by severity. Read-only. Use for /flue-loom:review, before a PR or deploy, or after flue-author writes code. ([discohead/flue-loom](https://github.com/discohead/flue-loom/blob/HEAD/agents/flue-reviewer.md))
- **fluency-author**: Authors a fluency (skills-practice) activity as a validated IR Activity JSON. Runs in its own context so fluency work never mixes with inquiry. Invoked by /build-lesson for genre=fluency. ([jcljest/isle-mlops](https://github.com/jcljest/isle-mlops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluency-author.md))
- **fluency-coach**: You are an AI Fluency Coach that helps users improve their AI collaboration skills. Your role is to observe AI interactions, identify opportunities for improvement, and provide constructive coaching feedback using the AI Fluency curriculum. ([leobessa/claude-plugins-ai-fluency](https://github.com/leobessa/claude-plugins-ai-fluency/blob/HEAD/agents/fluency-coach.md))

- **Fluent Theme Engineer**: Creates and manages custom Fluent 2 themes — brand ramp generation, token customization, Teams theme integration, dark/light/high-contrast support, and design system governance. &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants a custom brand theme user: "Create a Fluent theme with our company's brand color #E74C3C" assistant: "I'll use the Fluent Theme Engineer agent to generate a custom brand ramp and theme." &lt;commentary&gt; Custom theme creation with brand colors triggers this agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to customize tokens user: "How do I change the border radius and font in my… ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/fluent-ui-design/agents/fluent-theme-engineer.md) · ★19)
- **Fluent UI Designer**: Designs and architects UI layouts using the Microsoft Fluent 2 design system. Creates responsive, accessible interfaces with proper token usage, theming, and component selection. Provides design guidance for Teams apps, Copilot experiences, and general Microsoft-aligned web applications. &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to build a Teams app UI user: "Design a dashboard for my Teams tab app" assistant: "I'll use the Fluent UI Designer agent to architect a responsive dashboard layout." &lt;commentary&gt; UI design and layout architecture requests trigger this agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Co… ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/fluent-ui-design/agents/fluent-ui-designer.md) · ★19)
- **fluent-builder**: Runs FluentUI verification (check_all.py, MSBuild Debug + Release, FluentUITests.exe) and returns only errors, warnings and failing tests. Use after any code change before reporting it complete, and for mutation-test runs. ([lenovo-li/Fluent](https://github.com/lenovo-li/Fluent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-builder.md))
- **fluent-design-reviewer**: Reviews and audits an existing UI (web/React, Power BI theme, Power Apps/Pages, PCF, or a native iOS/Android/Windows app) against Fluent 2 guidelines, tokens, and accessibility. Reports high-confidence issues and concrete fixes. USE FOR: 'review my Fluent UI', 'is this Fluent 2 compliant', design/accessibility audit, token misuse, auditing Fluent 1 (v8) code. DO NOT USE FOR: building new UI (use the builder/specialists). - **fluent-doc-auditor**: Read-only audit that FluentUI documentation matches the code change — API sections vs. the changed headers, performance contracts, protected extension points — plus doc lifetime rules and project registration. Run after every code change, in parallel with fluent-reviewer; the Stop hook requires it. ([lenovo-li/Fluent](https://github.com/lenovo-li/Fluent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-doc-auditor.md))
- **fluent-expert**: ServiceNow Fluent expert. Writes and reviews .now.ts metadata (Table, BusinessRule, UiPage, RestApi, ScriptInclude, Acl, Property, Record, Flow…) and src/server Glide scripts in now-sdk projects. Use whenever a task touches a *.now.ts file, a now.config.json project, the @servicenow/sdk API surface, Now.ID / keys.ts record identity, or `now-sdk build|transform|dependencies`. Looks up the version-pinned SDK doc corpus instead of answering from memory. Does not deploy. ([tehubersheezy/sn-weekend-change-manager](https://github.com/tehubersheezy/sn-weekend-change-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-expert.md) · ★1)
- **fluent-explorer**: Read-only FluentUI researcher. Use before writing a new control or changing an existing one, to collect the real API (signatures, base classes, tokens, patterns) from the nearest existing controls and docs. Also for "where is X / how does Y work" questions spanning several files. ([lenovo-li/Fluent](https://github.com/lenovo-li/Fluent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-explorer.md))
- **fluent-migration-engineer**: Adopts/migrates an EXISTING app or report to Fluent 2 — Fluent UI React v8 to v9, from another design system (MUI/Chakra/Ant Design/Bootstrap), replacing hardcoded values with tokens, and per-surface (Power BI/Power Apps/Power Pages/PCF). USE FOR: migrate to Fluent 2, adopt Fluent 2 in an existing app, v8 to v9, replace design system with Fluent, restyle an existing report/app to Fluent 2. DO NOT USE FOR: greenfield builds (use fluent-web-engineer / fluent-ui-builder). ([ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin](https://github.com/ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluent-migration-engineer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fluent-native-engineer**: Builds Fluent UI on native platforms: iOS (SwiftUI/UIKit, fluentui-apple), Android (Jetpack Compose/Views, fluentui-android) and Windows (WinUI 3 / Windows App SDK, WinUI 2, WPF Fluent theme). Use for Fluent on iOS/Android/Windows, MSFAvatar or other MSF* types, tokenized.* Composables, WinUI 3 controls, WPF ThemeMode, native Fluent theming/brand color/dark mode. DO NOT USE FOR: web apps (use fluent-web-engineer), Power BI or Power Platform (use those specialists). ([ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin](https://github.com/ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluent-native-engineer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fluent-parity-researcher**: Use when a WaveUI phase spec, a roadmap item or a parity question needs Fluent UI React v9's actual API and behavior for a component — props and defaults, keyboard, ARIA, events and callback data, slots — compared with WaveUI's current code and mapped to the gap ids of docs/research/fluent-ui-v9-comparison.md. ([mortenbrudvik/waveui](https://github.com/mortenbrudvik/waveui/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-parity-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **fluent-power-platform-engineer**: Applies Fluent 2 across Microsoft Power Platform: Power Apps modern controls + modern theming, Power Pages styling with Fluent tokens, and PCF code components built with Fluent UI React v9 (or Fluent 8 via platform libraries). Use for Fluent in Power Apps, Power Pages Fluent look, or a Fluent PCF control. DO NOT USE FOR: standalone web apps (use fluent-web-engineer) or Power BI (use fluent-powerbi-designer). ([ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin](https://github.com/ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluent-power-platform-engineer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fluent-powerbi-designer**: Creates Fluent 2-aligned Power BI report themes (theme JSON with dataColors, textClasses, and visualStyles visual defaults) and scaffolds Fluent-themed PBIP/PBIR report projects. Also ADOPTS Fluent 2 into an existing PBIP/PBIR report and repairs the layout distortion the theme introduces (overlaps, clipped titles, out-of-bounds visuals, slicer and small-multiple reflow, broken bookmarks or navigation). Use for Power BI theme, report styling, visual defaults, PBIP/PBIR, applying Fluent 2 to an existing report, or making a report look like Fluent 2. DO NOT USE FOR: web apps or Power Apps/Pages. ([ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin](https://github.com/ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluent-powerbi-designer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fluent-reviewer**: Reviews a FluentUI diff it did not write, against the framework's non-negotiables and contracts. Use after implementing a change and after fluent-builder passes, before reporting the work complete. Pass the scope (branch, files, or "working tree"). ([lenovo-li/Fluent](https://github.com/lenovo-li/Fluent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-reviewer.md))
- **fluent-spa-designer**: Implement production Fluent UI in the current React web app. ([collisionengineers/collisionspike](https://github.com/collisionengineers/collisionspike/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluent-spa-designer.md) · 2 copies)
- **fluent-ui-builder**: Primary agent for designing and building Microsoft Fluent 2 (Fluent UI 2.0) experiences. Does the work and routes to specialists: Fluent web UIs (React v9 / Web Components), Power BI Fluent themes + PBIP/PBIR reports, Power Platform (Power Apps, Power Pages, PCF), native platforms (iOS, Android, Windows), Fluent 1 (Fluent UI React v8 / Office UI Fabric), and Figma design-to-code. USE FOR: build/design with Fluent, Fluent component, Fluent theme/tokens, Copilot/AI chat UI, Power BI Fluent theme, PBIP/PBIR report, Fluent in Power Apps/Power Pages, PCF with Fluent, Fluent on iOS/Android/Windows… ([ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin](https://github.com/ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluent-ui-builder.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fluent-web-engineer**: Builds Fluent 2 web UIs with Fluent UI React v9 (@fluentui/react-components) and Fluent Web Components v3 (@fluentui/web-components). Also works in Fluent 1 (Fluent UI React v8) codebases and turns Figma designs into Fluent code. Use for creating/modifying web apps, components, forms, layouts, and Copilot/AI chat surfaces styled with Fluent 2 tokens and theming. DO NOT USE FOR: Power BI or Power Platform (use those specialists), or native iOS/Android/Windows (use fluent-native-engineer). ([ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin](https://github.com/ArasaniRohithReddy/fluent-ui-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluent-web-engineer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **FluentUI**: Fluent UI specialist for design system compliance, component selection, theming, layout patterns, and accessible UI composition using Fluent UI React v9. ([E-G-C/dude](https://github.com/E-G-C/dude/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/dude-pack-fluent-ui-specialist.agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **fluff-lens**: Finds fluff in prose (defense phrases, redundant pairs, wordy constructions, empty qualifiers, hedges, work done for the reader). Report-only, used by /plainspoken:deep. ([cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools](https://github.com/cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/plainspoken/agents/fluff-lens.md))
- **fluid-dynamicist**: Reasons from Navier–Stokes and dimensionless scaling through RANS/LES/DNS selection, mesh/y+ strategy, OpenFOAM/Fluent workflows, and MMS + ASME V&V 20 / PIV validation. ([galeep/plugin-place](https://github.com/galeep/plugin-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/sci-agents-engineering/agents/fluid-dynamicist.md))
- **fluid-mechanics-engineer**: Reasons from Navier–Stokes reductions through Darcy–Weisbach/Crane TP-410 pipe networks, pump system curves, NPSH/affinity laws, HI turbomachinery selection, and ASME V&V 20 CFD validation when simulation supports design. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/blob/HEAD/scientific-agents/fluid-mechanics-engineer/agents/fluid-mechanics-engineer.md) · ★255 · 3 copies)
- **fluid-mechanics-engineer**: Fluid Mechanics Engineer: Reasons from Navier–Stokes reductions through Darcy–Weisbach/Crane TP-410 pipe networks, pump system curves, NPSH/affinity laws, HI turbomachinery selection, and ASME V&V 20 CFD validation when simulation supports design. - **fluid-pr-agent**: Executes Fluid test migration and coverage package-by-package, then returns a PR-ready handoff for wrapper-driven PR creation. ([hxrshxz/fluid-cloudnative-custom-agent-workflows](https://github.com/hxrshxz/fluid-cloudnative-custom-agent-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/fluid-pr-agent.md) · ★3)
- **fluid-pr-review-agent**: Handles post-PR bot review rounds for Fluid work with strict scope, safe auto-application, and fail-closed must-fix gating. ([hxrshxz/fluid-cloudnative-custom-agent-workflows](https://github.com/hxrshxz/fluid-cloudnative-custom-agent-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/fluid-pr-review-agent.md) · ★3)
- **fluid-sim-engineer**: Expert on real-time GPU fluid simulation — the Navier-Stokes pass pipeline, the simulation orchestrator, quality tiers, and splat/ambient/impulse physics. Builds AND reviews. Owns the PHYSICS passes (advect, curl, divergence, pressure, vorticity, splat) and the code that drives them. For the render shaders and print/aesthetic passes use shader-artisan; for frame budget and frametime measurement use webgl-perf-guardian. Use for: pipeline correctness, pressure-solve tuning, tier cost, splat feel, ambient motion rigs, and "the sim boils / explodes / ghosts / fades wrong" debugging. ([manu-brighter/claude-code-kit](https://github.com/manu-brighter/claude-code-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/creative-frontend/agents/fluid-sim-engineer.md))
- **fluid-specialist**: Owns fluid simulation — SPH, GPU acceleration. Use for work in docs/specs/physics/fluid.md. ([developerz-ai/nexus-engine](https://github.com/developerz-ai/nexus-engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fluid-specialist.md))
- **fluig-deployer**: Executa deploy de artefatos Fluig no servidor via SSH ou REST API. Suporta dois modos SSH (copia + reinicia container) e REST (upload via API Fluig com OAuth2). Requer confirmação antes de deploy em produção. Reporta resultado ao team lead. ([dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/fluig/agents/fluig-deployer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **fluig-escritor**: Escritor Haiku para projetos TOTVS Fluig. Recebe o caminho de um plano-*.md e escreve só os steps marcados [escritor] — formulário (HTML, custom.js, custom_valida.js, zoom.js, ajuda_config.js, eventos do form) e cópias idênticas de arquivos padrão — quando o plano já traz tudo (campos, tabelas, código). Não decide nada, não mexe em script de workflow, dataset ou diagrama, não publica, não faz commit. Use a partir do orquestrador, em paralelo com o fluig-executor. ([antoniosdn/fluig-plugin](https://github.com/antoniosdn/fluig-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluig-escritor.md) · ★3)
- **fluig-executor**: Executor Sonnet para projetos TOTVS Fluig. Recebe o caminho de um plano-*.md aprovado, implementa os steps [executor] exatamente como escritos (script de workflow, dataset, diagrama/regeneração; formulário só se o plano não for marcado ou o escritor devolver pendente), publica com fluigcli quando o plano manda e devolve um relatório curto. Os steps [escritor] são do fluig-escritor (Haiku), em paralelo. Não planeja, não escolhe atividade/gateway/campo/dataset, não amplia escopo, não faz commit. Use a partir do orquestrador (sessão principal), depois de o usuário aprovar o plano. ([antoniosdn/fluig-plugin](https://github.com/antoniosdn/fluig-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluig-executor.md) · ★3)
- **fluig-implementer**: Implementa tarefas do plano Fluig seguindo TDD. Recebe task via SendMessage, implementa, testa, faz self-review e reporta status. Modelo sonnet — implementação exige raciocínio sobre regra de negócio. Comunicação bidirecional com reviewers. Use proactively após /fluig:plan. ([dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/fluig/agents/fluig-implementer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **fluig-planejador**: Planejador Opus para projetos TOTVS Fluig. Recebe a tarefa (importar, ajustar ou criar processo/formulário/dataset) e a raiz do projeto, faz o levantamento nos fontes e no servidor (só leitura), grava o plano-*.md que o fluig-escritor (steps [escritor]) e o fluig-executor (steps [executor]) seguem sem decidir e devolve um resumo curto ao orquestrador. Também ajusta o plano (itens [plano] do revisor, defeitos de teste). Não fala com o usuário, não implementa, não publica. Use a partir do orquestrador (sessão principal), para manter o levantamento fora do contexto dela. ([antoniosdn/fluig-plugin](https://github.com/antoniosdn/fluig-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluig-planejador.md) · ★3)
- **fluig-qa**: Analisa qualidade de artefatos Fluig identificando casos de borda não tratados, campos obrigatórios sem validação, datasets sem constraints de filtro, e verifica se widgets têm cobertura de testes adequada. Foca em QA antes do deploy. Comunicação bidirecional com team. ([dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/fluig/agents/fluig-qa.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **fluig-reviewer**: Revisa código Fluig (datasets, formulários, workflows, widgets) verificando nomenclatura, tratamento de erros, segurança, padrões de API e boas práticas. Relatório CRÍTICO/AVISO/SUGESTÃO. Pode enviar feedback direto ao implementador. ([dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/fluig/agents/fluig-reviewer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **fluig-revisor**: Revisor Opus para projetos TOTVS Fluig. Compara o git diff com o plano-*.md e responde um checklist objetivo (escopo, fluxo × servidor, campos × HTML, validação, Rhino ES5, APIs do Fluig, quebras de linha, publicação). Somente leitura; devolve APROVADO ou REPROVADO com itens numerados e a origem de cada um (execução ou plano). Use depois do fluig-escritor/fluig-executor e, fora do fluxo, para revisar mudança média antes do teste do usuário (contra a descrição do pedido, quando não houver plano). ([antoniosdn/fluig-plugin](https://github.com/antoniosdn/fluig-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fluig-revisor.md) · ★3)
- **fluig-spec-reviewer**: Verifica se a implementação corresponde exatamente à especificação da task. Lê o código real, não confia no relatório do implementer. Retorna ✅ ou ❌ com referências file:line. Pode enviar feedback direto ao implementador. ([dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/dataagile/dataagile-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/fluig/agents/fluig-spec-reviewer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **fluig-test-auditor**: Auditor Opus do orquestrador /fluig-teste. Recebe a suíte testes/&lt;processo&gt;.json e os arquivos de resultado da rodada, confere no servidor (só leitura) e no código do processo, classifica cada falha como defeito do processo ou defeito do teste e, para cada defeito do processo, escreve um rascunho de plano de correção. - **fluig-test-runner**: Executor Sonnet do orquestrador /fluig-teste.
